# Codex — answer batch I.2 paper-vs-script questions

You are being asked to act as the math authority on the moving-throat PDE project. The v2 paper-grounded red-team audit pass on batch I.2 (stages 013-023) surfaced 6 questions where the scripts and the paper disagree, and the user wants your recommendations.

## What's going on

The PDE ledger project is in a script-verification phase ("red-team audit pipeline"). Each stage NNN has:

- A paper card at `paper/stages/stage_NNN.tex`
- Optional source notes at `notes/stages/moving_throat_pde_stage{NNN}_*.md` (for the EM-projected linear stages 013-020, no per-stage notes exist — the paper card + appendix row + the original `notes/em_projected/` derivation scripts are the source)
- A SymPy audit script at `scripts/moving_throat_pde_stage{NNN}_*.py`
- A Mathematica audit script at `mathematica/moving_throat_pde_stage{NNN}_*.wl`

The v2 audit reads the paper FIRST, builds a model of what the stage is supposed to prove, then checks whether the scripts faithfully verify that. When the script's assertion and the paper's claim disagree, that's a `paper_misalignment` finding routed to the user for resolution — and you're the user's math advisor for these decisions.

Per-stage red-team audit reports for the 11 stages in batch I.2 live at `redteam/reports/stage_013.md` through `redteam/reports/stage_023.md`. The full directives (which include the questions you'll be answering, plus the auditor's reasoning) are at `redteam/directives/stage_NNN.md`. You don't have to read all of these, but they're available if you want the full context for a question.

## Batch I.2 context

Batch I.2 is the back half of the linear projected-EM core range (stages 004-021 plus 022-023 grouped-bundle bookends). Stages 013-020 are file-for-file SymPy migrations of the original `notes/em_projected/step_NN_*` derivation scripts; the paper cards under `paper/stages/` were written later as compact summaries. The audit pattern is dominated by `paper_missing_script_claim`: the scripts verify more than the paper cards declare. This is the same pattern as batch I.1.

Three exceptions worth noting:
- Stage 015 has a missing notes file (sympy docstring references `step_13_parent_throat_action_master_notes.md` which does not exist in the repository).
- Stage 018 similarly references nonexistent `step_16_parent_throat_action_bundle_master_notes.md`.
- Stage 021 is the inverse direction: the paper Output enumerates three exports, but the scripts only assert two — the third (composed wall-level odd quadrupole coefficient) is computed but not asserted against the paper's specific closed value.

## Your task

Open this questions file:

  `redteam/resolutions/batch_I2_paper_alignment.md`

There are 6 questions. For each question:

1. Read the question's file:line references and open the relevant paper card + script files.
2. Decide which option (a, b, c, or skip) is correct, based on what the math actually requires.
3. Fill in the `## Recommendation` block with:

   ```yaml
   direction: a       # or b, c, skip
   rationale: |
     1-3 sentences explaining the choice. Cite specific file:line references where helpful.
   ```

When you're done with all 6, append a `summary:` line at the end of the questions file noting `X answered, Y skipped`.

## Important rules

- **Only edit the questions file** at `redteam/resolutions/batch_I2_paper_alignment.md`. Do NOT touch any script, paper, or notes file. Recommendations are applied by a separate workflow after the user reviews them.
- **If you do not have a high-confidence answer for a question, write `direction: skip`** with a one-line note explaining why. Do NOT guess. The user will review skipped questions manually.
- **Read upstream/downstream stages if needed.** Some questions depend on conventions set elsewhere — Q1 (stage 013's `deltaP_2/deltaP_4` and sieve) depends on what stage 014 formally owns; Q3 (stage 015's wall-only/Y20/grouped blocks) needs you to check whether stages 016, 017, 020, 022, or 023 already own the same content; Q4 (stage 018's extra families) might belong in stage 021. Open the relevant paper cards as needed.
- **The earlier red-team audits already verified the algebra holds together internally** — these questions are specifically about claims that the scripts make vs. claims the paper makes, where one side may be wrong relative to the other or where the paper card may need to grow / shrink.
- **Don't edit scripts or paper.** Your recommendations will be applied by a separate Codex apply workflow after the user reviews them.
- **Iterate as needed.** If reading a stage's notes reveals you need to check a sibling stage, do it. The end goal is high-confidence recommendations or honest "skip" markers.

## Project memory worth knowing

- The Mathematica `.wl` files live in `mathematica/`, NOT in `scripts/`. SymPy `.py` files live in `scripts/`.
- The project framing is a "toy superfluid analog" aiming to unify GR-like and EM-like derivations. In private/internal context (which this is), engaging with the "unification" language directly is fine. (Public paper framing uses strict toy-analog language only.)
- The project is moving toward an end-to-end second pass, so accuracy of these recommendations matters — the first pass result is what the second pass will check against.
- The same pattern in batch I.1 was resolved as: Q1=a, Q2=b, Q3=c, Q4=a, Q5=c, Q6=a, Q7=c — a mix of expand-paper, trim-script, and add-acknowledgement directions based on what the math actually owned. Use that as a reference for the kind of judgment expected, but answer I.2 on its own merits.

## When done

Append the summary line at the bottom of the questions file (`summary: X answered, Y skipped`) and exit. Do not modify any other file.

# Batch I.2 paper-misalignment resolutions

Six v2 audits surfaced `paper_misalignment` findings. Each is described below with the relevant file:line evidence and (a/b/c/skip) options. Codex: please read the cited paper and script files, then fill the empty `## Recommendation` block under each question with `direction: a|b|c|skip` and a one-paragraph rationale citing file:line. If you do not have a high-confidence answer for a question, write `direction: skip`.

This batch's pattern (like I.1) is dominated by `paper_missing_script_claim`: the EM-projected scripts (stages 013-020) were ported from `notes/em_projected/`, but the paper cards under `paper/stages/` were written later as compact summaries. The natural directions are (a) expand paper card to enumerate the extra script checks; (b) trim scripts to paper scope and migrate extras to whatever stage actually owns them; or (c) add a light paper acknowledgement.

---

## Q1 — Stage 013 (paper_missing_script_claim, low)

**Files:**
- paper: `paper/stages/stage_013.tex` (whole card; n_0 only; no `deltaP_2/deltaP_4/n_2/n_4` mention)
- sympy: `scripts/moving_throat_pde_stage013_projected_maxwell_mouth_taylor_master_sympy_audit.py:106-151`
- mathematica: `mathematica/moving_throat_pde_stage013_projected_maxwell_mouth_taylor_master_mathematica_audit.wl:126-166`
- appendix row: `paper/appendices/stage_appendix_part01.tex` row for 013 ("Mouth-local Taylor map for projected source and flux corrections" — too broad to anchor specific deliverables)

**What the script verifies that the paper does not declare:**

1. `deltaP_2` and `deltaP_4` defined and tested via substantive identities `∂(deltaP_2)/∂G_w' = -2P/(D_0*Delta^2)` and a nonzero `∂(deltaP_4)/∂G_w'`. Neither symbol appears in the paper card.
2. "Mechanism sieve": 2x2 linear-solve argument that `(K_1, H_even)|_{Sx=Hx=0}` and `(K_1, H_even)|_{Qx=Dx=0}` admit only the trivial pair. No paper-side mention.
3. `n_2, n_4` derived in Mathematica and asserted in SymPy. Paper card mentions only `n_0`.

**Options:**

- (a) Expand `paper/stages/stage_013.tex` Output to declare `deltaP_2, deltaP_4, n_2, n_4` and the mechanism sieve as deliverables of stage 013.
- (b) Trim the scripts to the four equation-numbered deliverables (z_0, z_2, z_4, n_0) and move the `deltaP` / sieve / `n_2 / n_4` machinery to stage 014 (where K_1, H_even are formally defined).
- (c) Add 1-2 sentences to the paper card explicitly acknowledging that the audit also exercises the secondary Taylor data that feeds stage 014.
- (skip) Codex does not have a high-confidence answer.

## Q3 — Stage 015 (paper_missing_script_claim, high)

**Files:**
- paper: `paper/stages/stage_015.tex:44-46` (Output paragraph), and whole card
- appendix row: `paper/appendices/stage_appendix_part01.tex:52` ("Parent throat-action packet and projection/reduction status boundary")

- mathematica: `mathematica/moving_throat_pde_stage015_parent_throat_action_master_mathematica_audit.wl:104-196`
- sympy docstring (line 2) references nonexistent `step_13_parent_throat_action_master_notes.md`

**What the script verifies that the paper does not declare:**

Paper card Output says only the parent throat action promotion and the K_eta exact-quadratic-recovery formula. Yet ~6 of 12 sympy asserts (A9-A19) and ~21 of 28 mathematica checks (M8-M28) cover:

1. Wall-only specializations of full even-channel gates `K_1 = -dM + dK/9` and `H_even = (2/3) dM - dK/27`, with substitution `{B_{01}, B_{21}, B_{41}, Z_{01}, Z_{21}, Z_{41}} → 0` (sympy:123 / math:109), Jacobian determinant `1/27` (sympy:174 / math:151), Gaussian overlap integrals `dMoverlap = √(π/3)` and `dKoverlap = 23√π/(3√3)` (math:126-127).
2. Real-Y20 overlap ratios `{1, 1/2, -1}` for `m = 0, 1, 2` (sympy:195-200 / math:175-184).
3. Grouped trace identities `xbar = x0`, `bx = 3*ax` with `lam20=1, lam21=1/2, lam22=-1` (sympy:202-208 / math:186-196).

**Severity is "high"** because this is roughly half of each script — large fraction of script effort with no paper anchor.

**Options:**

- (a) Expand `paper/stages/stage_015.tex` to declare wall-only gates, Y20 ratios, and grouped trace as additional deliverables of stage 015, and restore (or rewrite) `notes/stages/moving_throat_pde_stage015_*.md` to back them.
- (b) Trim scripts to A1-A8 / M1-M7 (only the K_eta exact-quadratic block, which is the deliverable the paper actually claims); migrate the wall-only/Y20/grouped blocks to whatever stage(s) genuinely own them.

## Q4 — Stage 018 (paper_missing_script_claim, medium)

**Files:**
- paper: `paper/stages/stage_018.tex:26-28` (Output paragraph), body 14-22 (only M_Σ, K_Σ branch integrals)
- appendix row 58: "Bundle-level parent-action identities used by the projected electromagnetic response" (plural "identities" hints at more, never enumerated)
- sympy: `scripts/moving_throat_pde_stage018_parent_throat_action_bundle_master_sympy_audit.py:1-2, 25-88`
- mathematica: `mathematica/moving_throat_pde_stage018_parent_throat_action_bundle_master_mathematica_audit.wl:1-15, 24-122`
- sympy docstring references nonexistent `step_16_parent_throat_action_bundle_master_notes.md`

**What the script verifies that the paper does not declare:**

Paper Output says only M_Σ, K_Σ branch integrals. Scripts additionally verify:

1. An algebraic identity about coefficients of a quartic pole expansion (one-pole numerator).
2. Two `KSigma` closure conditions and their compatibility (one-pole vs. normalization closures).
3. A 2x2 even-gate determinant equal to `1/27` and closed-form wall-stiffness/wall-inertia slopes.
4. A residual `Xi1` after applying those slopes.

Only the trailing Gaussian integrals A18/A19 / M8 directly correspond to the paper's M_Σ, K_Σ deliverables, and they do so only for a specific concrete profile (`μ_η = T_w = K_η + 6 T_Ω = 1`, `β = exp(-w²/2)`).

- (a) Expand the paper card and restore `notes/stages/moving_throat_pde_stage018_*.md` to declare the four extra claim families (one-pole closure, gate determinant, wall slopes, Xi_1 residual) as 018 deliverables.
- (b) Move the four extra claim families to a different stage (candidates: 015-017 or 021 — "Reduced Maxwell/mixed one-port normal form"); script becomes just the bridge identity check.
- (c) Prune scripts to just the M_Σ, K_Σ bridge identity (drop families 1-4 entirely).
- (skip) Codex does not have a high-confidence answer.

## Q5 — Stage 020 (paper_missing_script_claim, medium)

**What the script verifies that the paper does not declare:**

Both engines compute and assert Y20-squared lane overlap ratios `λ_{20} = 1, λ_{21} = 1/2, λ_{22} = -1` and vanishing of same-sign m-cross terms. The paper card and appendix row make no reference to spherical harmonics, Y20 overlaps, Gaunt integrals, or these lane ratios. Paper card content is purely the 1D wall-slope solve in the `β_2(w)` profile and identities `eq:stage020-wall-slopes` through `eq:stage020-residual-xi`.

The "weak-axisymmetric" content of the stage title suggests the Y20 ratios are conceptually load-bearing somewhere — but the paper does not say where.

**Options:**

- (a) Amend `paper/stages/stage_020.tex` to add a paragraph or equation citing the Y20 lane ratios as part of the weak-axisymmetric deliverable.
- (b) Remove the Y20 block from both scripts (sympy:45-50, wl:34-67) — they belong to a different stage.
- (skip) Codex does not have a high-confidence answer.

## Q6 — Stage 021 (script_missing_paper_claim, medium)

**This is the inverse direction**: paper claims more than scripts assert.

**Files:**
- paper: `paper/stages/stage_021.tex:77-81` (Output paragraph)
- paper: `paper/stages/stage_021.tex:71-75` (`eq:app-stage021-wall-odd`)
- sympy: `scripts/moving_throat_pde_stage021_reduced_one_port_normal_form_sympy_audit.py:238-244` (`Dcorr` computed and printed, no assertion)
- mathematica: `mathematica/moving_throat_pde_stage021_reduced_one_port_normal_form_mathematica_audit.wl:135, 140` (`dCorr` computed and printed, no assertion)

**What the paper claims that the script does not verify:**

The paper's Output paragraph explicitly enumerates three exports: (1) reduced one-port self-energy, (2) transfer factor, (3) **wall-level outgoing quadrupole coefficient** `δD_2^(odd)(ω) = -i N_2(0) (a^5/(27 c_s^5)) ω^5 + O(ω^7)`. The numeric coefficient `a^5/(27 c_s^5)` is substituted in for `Γ_5^port`.

In both scripts, Section III computes:
```
Dcorr = -I * Gamma_port * omega^5 * N0   (sympy)
dCorr = -I * gammaPort * omega^5 * n0    (mathematica)
```
`Gamma_port` is a free symbolic constant; it is never specialized to `a^5/(27 c_s^5)` and `Dcorr` is never asserted against the paper's closed form. Sections III and IV separately verify N_l(0) and Γ_5^port; the composition is asserted only in prose, not in code.

The fix is algebraically trivial (one substitution + assertion in each script).

**Options:**

- (a) Add the composed assertion to both scripts (sympy and mathematica) — paper is right, scripts have a verification gap. **Recommended:** the auditor flagged paper-says-three-the-scripts-prove-two-and-a-half as the natural read.
- (b) Trim the paper Output to two exports (drop the wall-level odd-coefficient claim) — the composition is trivial enough that the paper can describe it in prose without claiming it as a deliverable.
- (skip) Codex does not have a high-confidence answer.

## Recommendation

direction:
rationale:

     8	def assert_zero(label: str, expr: sp.Expr) -> None:
     9	    residue = sp.factor(sp.together(sp.simplify(expr)))
    10	    if residue != 0:
    11	        raise AssertionError(f"{label} failed: {sp.sstr(residue)}")
    12	
    13	
    14	def assert_nonzero(label: str, expr: sp.Expr) -> None:
    15	    residue = sp.factor(sp.together(sp.simplify(expr)))
    16	    if residue == 0:
    17	        raise AssertionError(f"{label} unexpectedly vanished")
    18	
    19	
    20	def main() -> None:
    21	    u, ell = sp.symbols("u ell", positive=True)
    22	    X0, X1, X2 = sp.symbols("X0 X1 X2")
    23	    W = sp.exp(-u)
    24	    X = X0 + ell * u * X1 + ell**2 * u**2 * X2 / 2
    25	    Xproj = sp.integrate(W * X, (u, 0, sp.oo))
    26	    assert_zero("one-sided Taylor first moment", sp.series(Xproj, ell, 0, 2).removeO() - (X0 + ell * X1))

    29	        sp.series(Xproj, ell, 0, 2).removeO() - (X0 + 2 * ell * X1),

    35	    assert_zero("one-sided Taylor arbitrary first moment", sp.series(Xproj_W2, ell, 0, 2).removeO() - (X0 + ell * mu1_W2 * X1))

    64	        + 2 * Delta * Hport * S2 * d1
    65	        + 2 * Delta * Q * S2 * s1
    66	        + 2 * Delta * Q * d1
    67	        + Delta * S2**2 * q1
    68	        - 3 * Q * S2**2 * d1
    69	    ) / Delta**4
    70	
    71	    # n0, n2, n4 are the analogous order-ell^0, ell^2, ell^4
    72	    # coefficients of the chain-rule expansion
    73	    # (P(t) - Gw(t)*ell^2)^2/(Delta(t) - S2(t)*ell^2 + ell^4)^2,
    74	    # evaluated at t=0 with leading values {P, Delta, S2, Gw} and
    75	    # t-derivatives {p1, d1, s1, g1}. The polynomial forms below are
    76	    # independently derived and asserted against the master primitive in
    77	    # mathematica/moving_throat_pde_stage013_projected_maxwell_mouth_taylor_master_mathematica_audit.wl
    78	    # (claim M4); a discrepancy there is the canonical place to discover
    79	    # an error in these literals.
    80	    n0 = 2 * P * (Delta * p1 - P * d1) / Delta**3
    81	    n2 = -(
    82	        2 * Delta**2 * (Gw * p1 + P * g1)
    83	        - 2 * Delta * P * (2 * Gw * d1 + P * s1 + 2 * S2 * p1)
    84	        + 6 * P**2 * S2 * d1
    85	    ) / Delta**4
    86	    n4 = 2 * (
    87	        Delta**3 * Gw * g1
    88	        - Delta**2 * Gw**2 * d1
    89	        - 2 * Delta**2 * Gw * P * s1
    90	        - 2 * Delta**2 * Gw * S2 * p1
    91	        - 2 * Delta**2 * P * S2 * g1
    92	        - 2 * Delta**2 * P * p1
    93	        + 6 * Delta * Gw * P * S2 * d1
    94	        + 3 * Delta * P**2 * S2 * s1
    95	        + 3 * Delta * P**2 * d1
    96	        + 3 * Delta * P * S2**2 * p1
    97	        - 6 * P**2 * S2**2 * d1
    98	    ) / Delta**5
    99	
   100	    Xi = sp.simplify((2 * p1 / P - 2 * d1 / Delta + q1 / (D0 * Delta) - Q * d1 / (D0 * Delta**2)).subs(subs_der) / mu1)
   101	    K1 = sp.simplify((-(z2 + z0 / sp.Integer(9))).subs(subs_der) / mu1)
   102	    H_even = sp.simplify(((-z4 + sp.Rational(2, 3) * z2 - z0 / sp.Integer(27))).subs(subs_der) / mu1)
   103	
   104	    assert_zero("dXi/dPprime", sp.diff(Xi, Px) - 2 / P)
   105	
   106	    deltaP2 = sp.simplify((D0**2 * n2 - 2 * D0 * D2 * n0 + 2 * D0 * N0 * z2 - 2 * D2 * N0 * z0) / D0**3)
   107	    deltaP4 = sp.simplify(
   108	        (
   109	            D0**3 * n4
   110	            - 2 * D0**2 * D2 * n2
   111	            - 2 * D0**2 * D4 * n0
   112	            + 2 * D0**2 * N0 * z4
   113	            + 3 * D0 * D2**2 * n0
   114	            - 2 * D0 * D2 * N0 * z2
   115	            - 2 * D0 * D4 * N0 * z0
   116	            + 2 * D2**2 * N0 * z0
   117	        )
   118	        / D0**4
   119	    )
   120	    deltaP2_der = sp.simplify(deltaP2.subs(subs_der) / mu1)
   121	    deltaP4_der = sp.simplify(deltaP4.subs(subs_der) / mu1)
   122	    assert_zero("d(delta P2)/dGprime", sp.diff(deltaP2_der, Gx) + 2 * P / (D0 * Delta**2))
   123	    if sp.simplify(sp.diff(deltaP4_der, Gx)) == 0:
   124	        raise AssertionError("delta P4 should depend on G_W prime")
   125	
   126	    qd_only = sp.solve(
   127	        [sp.Eq(K1.subs({Sx: 0, Hx: 0}), 0), sp.Eq(H_even.subs({Sx: 0, Hx: 0}), 0)],
   128	        [Qx, Dx],
   129	        dict=True,
   130	    )
   131	    sh_only = sp.solve(
   132	        [sp.Eq(K1.subs({Qx: 0, Dx: 0}), 0), sp.Eq(H_even.subs({Qx: 0, Dx: 0}), 0)],
   133	        [Sx, Hx],
   134	        dict=True,
   135	    )
   136	    qd_matrix = sp.Matrix([
   137	        [sp.diff(K1.subs({Sx: 0, Hx: 0}), Qx), sp.diff(K1.subs({Sx: 0, Hx: 0}), Dx)],
   138	        [sp.diff(H_even.subs({Sx: 0, Hx: 0}), Qx), sp.diff(H_even.subs({Sx: 0, Hx: 0}), Dx)],
   139	    ])
   140	    sh_matrix = sp.Matrix([
   141	        [sp.diff(K1.subs({Qx: 0, Dx: 0}), Sx), sp.diff(K1.subs({Qx: 0, Dx: 0}), Hx)],
   142	        [sp.diff(H_even.subs({Qx: 0, Dx: 0}), Sx), sp.diff(H_even.subs({Qx: 0, Dx: 0}), Hx)],
   143	    ])
   144	    assert_nonzero("Xi should depend on Pprime", sp.diff(Xi, Px))
   145	    assert_nonzero("deltaP4 should depend on G_W prime", sp.diff(deltaP4_der, Gx))
   146	    assert_nonzero("source/denominator sieve determinant", qd_matrix.det())
   147	    assert_nonzero("spectral sieve determinant", sh_matrix.det())
   148	    if qd_only != [{Qx: 0, Dx: 0}]:
   149	        raise AssertionError(f"Unexpected pure source/denominator solve: {qd_only}")
   150	    if sh_only != [{Sx: 0, Hx: 0}]:
   151	        raise AssertionError(f"Unexpected pure spectral solve: {sh_only}")
   152	
   153	    print("STEP 11 PROJECTED MAXWELL MOUTH-TAYLOR MASTER AUDIT")
   154	    print("Checked one-sided Taylor projection, bottleneck dependencies, G_W transport entry, and mechanism sieve.")
   155	    print("STATUS: PASS")
   156	
   157	
   158	if __name__ == "__main__":
   159	    main()

     4	\stagefield{Part / anchor}{Part I; primary anchor \resultanchor{MTDC-T4}.}
     5	\claimstatus{This stage is \StatusExact{} for the displayed algebraic identities inside the declared parent/projection data, with \StatusReduced{} or \StatusExactClosure{} inherited where the local text invokes the matched reduction, mouth-kernel closure, parent-action packet, or one-port normal form.}
     6	
     7	\paragraph{Source and audit.}
     8	This stage corresponds to
     9	\StageFile{scripts/moving_throat_pde_stage014_projected_maxwell_mouth_taylor_gate_bridge_sympy_audit.py}.
    10	It turns the mouth-Taylor primitive map into a gate-level compensation test.
    11	
    12	\paragraph{Even gates.}
    13	The conservative mouth shifts enter
    14	\begin{equation}
    15	\label{eq:stage014-k1}
    16	K_1=-z_2-\frac{z_0}{9},
    17	\end{equation}
    18	\begin{equation}
    19	\label{eq:stage014-heven}
    20	H_{\rm even}=-z_4+\frac23z_2-\frac{z_0}{27}.
    21	\end{equation}
    22	Solving the pair of even gates against the primitive derivative slots gives a
    23	nontrivial compensation denominator.  In the primitive notation of the audit,
    24	the determinant is proportional to
    25	\begin{equation}
    26	\label{eq:stage007-compensation-denominator}
    27	Q\,S_2-\Delta H_{\rm port}.
    28	\end{equation}
    29	
    30	\paragraph{Output.}
    31	Stage~014 exports the mechanism sieve: projected EM mouth data can tune
    32	the even gates only away from the degeneracy
    33	\eqref{eq:stage007-compensation-denominator}.

/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba scripts/moving_throat_pde_stage014_projected_maxwell_mouth_taylor_gate_bridge_sympy_audit.py | sed -n '1,180p'" in /var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""
     3	Projected Maxwell → mouth-Taylor → 5PN gate bridge.
     4	
     5	This script starts from the previously derived one-port projected-Maxwell primitive
     6	bridge and adds a concrete near-mouth Taylor ansatz:
     7	
     8	    X_proj(ell) = X_0 + ell * mu1 * X_0' + O(ell^2)
     9	
    10	for the primitive one-port packet X in {Q, S2, H_port, Delta, P, G_W}.
    11	
    12	It then pushes those slippages into the moving-throat 5PN bottlenecks
    13	
    14	    Xi_load = P1/P0,
    15	    K1      = D21 + D01/9,
    16	    H_even  = D41 - (2/3) D21 - D01/27,
    17	
    18	and the constant-prefactor transport slots delta P2, delta P4.
    19	"""
    20	
    21	import sympy as sp
    22	
    23	
    24	def assert_zero(label, expr):
    25	    residue = sp.factor(sp.together(sp.simplify(expr)))
    26	    if residue != 0:
    27	        raise AssertionError(f"{label} failed: {sp.sstr(residue)}")
    28	
    29	
    30	def assert_nonzero(label, expr):
    31	    residue = sp.factor(sp.together(sp.simplify(expr)))
    32	    if residue == 0:
    33	        raise AssertionError(f"{label} unexpectedly vanished")
    34	
    35	
    36	# Base one-port variables
    37	Q, S2, Hport, Delta, P, Gw = sp.symbols('Q S2 H Delta P Gw', nonzero=True)
    38	D0, D2, D4, N0, Ptarget = sp.symbols('D0 D2 D4 N0 Ptarget', nonzero=True)
    39	mu1 = sp.symbols('mu1')
    40	
    41	# Primitive slippages
    42	q1, s1, h1, d1, p1, g1 = sp.symbols('q1 s1 h1 d1 p1 g1')
    43	
    44	# Mouth derivatives
    45	Qx, Sx, Hx, Dx, Px, Gx = sp.symbols('Qx S2x Hx Deltax Px Gwx')
    46	
    47	# Primitive one-port formulas
    48	z0 = (Delta*q1 - Q*d1)/Delta**2
    49	z2 = (-Delta**2*h1 + Delta*(Hport*d1 + Q*s1 + S2*q1) - 2*Q*S2*d1)/Delta**3
    50	z4 = (-Delta**2*Hport*s1 - Delta**2*S2*h1 - Delta**2*q1 + 2*Delta*Hport*S2*d1 + 2*Delta*Q*S2*s1 + 2*Delta*Q*d1 + Delta*S2**2*q1 - 3*Q*S2**2*d1)/Delta**4
    51	
    52	n0 = 2*P*(Delta*p1 - P*d1)/Delta**3
    53	n2 = -(2*Delta**2*(Gw*p1 + P*g1) - 2*Delta*P*(2*Gw*d1 + P*s1 + 2*S2*p1) + 6*P**2*S2*d1)/Delta**4
    54	n4 = 2*(Delta**3*Gw*g1 - Delta**2*Gw**2*d1 - 2*Delta**2*Gw*P*s1 - 2*Delta**2*Gw*S2*p1 - 2*Delta**2*P*S2*g1 - 2*Delta**2*P*p1 + 6*Delta*Gw*P*S2*d1 + 3*Delta*P**2*S2*s1 + 3*Delta*P**2*d1 + 3*Delta*P*S2**2*p1 - 6*P**2*S2**2*d1)/Delta**5
    55	
    56	# Mouth-local one-sided Taylor ansatz
    57	subs_der = {q1: mu1*Qx, s1: mu1*Sx, h1: mu1*Hx, d1: mu1*Dx, p1: mu1*Px, g1: mu1*Gx}
    58	
    59	z0d = sp.simplify(z0.subs(subs_der)/mu1)
    60	z2d = sp.simplify(z2.subs(subs_der)/mu1)
    61	z4d = sp.simplify(z4.subs(subs_der)/mu1)
    62	n0d = sp.simplify(n0.subs(subs_der)/mu1)
    63	n2d = sp.simplify(n2.subs(subs_der)/mu1)
    64	n4d = sp.simplify(n4.subs(subs_der)/mu1)
    65	
    66	# 5PN bottlenecks
    67	Xi = sp.simplify((2*p1/P - 2*d1/Delta + q1/(D0*Delta) - Q*d1/(D0*Delta**2)).subs(subs_der)/mu1)
    68	K1 = sp.simplify((-(z2 + z0/sp.Integer(9))).subs(subs_der)/mu1)
    69	He = sp.simplify((-(z4) + sp.Rational(2,3)*z2 - z0/sp.Integer(27)).subs(subs_der)/mu1)
    70	
    71	S, T = sp.symbols('S T', nonzero=True)
    72	Compat = sp.simplify(((n0/Ptarget) - 6*S*z2/T + 3*S**2*z4/T**2).subs(subs_der)/mu1)
    73	
    74	deltaP2 = sp.simplify(((D0**2*n2 - 2*D0*D2*n0 + 2*D0*N0*z2 - 2*D2*N0*z0)/D0**3).subs(subs_der)/mu1)
    75	deltaP4 = sp.simplify(((D0**3*n4 - 2*D0**2*D2*n2 - 2*D0**2*D4*n0 + 2*D0**2*N0*z4 + 3*D0*D2**2*n0 - 2*D0*D2*N0*z2 - 2*D0*D4*N0*z0 + 2*D2**2*N0*z0)/D0**4).subs(subs_der)/mu1)
    76	
    77	# Mechanism sieve
    78	qd_only = sp.solve([sp.Eq(K1.subs({Sx:0, Hx:0}), 0), sp.Eq(He.subs({Sx:0, Hx:0}), 0)], [Qx, Dx], dict=True)
    79	sh_only = sp.solve([sp.Eq(K1.subs({Qx:0, Dx:0}), 0), sp.Eq(He.subs({Qx:0, Dx:0}), 0)], [Sx, Hx], dict=True)
    80	comp_surface = sp.solve([sp.Eq(K1, 0), sp.Eq(He, 0)], [Hx, Sx], dict=True)[0]
    81	
    82	qd_matrix = sp.Matrix([
    83	    [sp.diff(K1.subs({Sx: 0, Hx: 0}), Qx), sp.diff(K1.subs({Sx: 0, Hx: 0}), Dx)],
    84	    [sp.diff(He.subs({Sx: 0, Hx: 0}), Qx), sp.diff(He.subs({Sx: 0, Hx: 0}), Dx)],
    85	])
    86	sh_matrix = sp.Matrix([
    87	    [sp.diff(K1.subs({Qx: 0, Dx: 0}), Sx), sp.diff(K1.subs({Qx: 0, Dx: 0}), Hx)],
    88	    [sp.diff(He.subs({Qx: 0, Dx: 0}), Sx), sp.diff(He.subs({Qx: 0, Dx: 0}), Hx)],
    89	])
    90	Hx_den = sp.factor(sp.denom(sp.together(comp_surface[Hx])))
    91	Sx_den = sp.factor(sp.denom(sp.together(comp_surface[Sx])))
    92	
    93	coef_Xi_Px = sp.simplify(sp.diff(Xi, Px))
    94	coef_dP2_Gx = sp.simplify(sp.diff(deltaP2, Gx))
    95	coef_dP4_Gx = sp.simplify(sp.diff(deltaP4, Gx))
    96	
    97	# z0 and n0 are single primitive monomials, so their lifts are structural
    98	# (z0d, n0d are *defined* on lines 59, 62 by exactly the substitution).
    99	# Real derivative-map content lives in z2d, z4d, n2d, n4d, where multiple
   100	# primitive slips cross-contract. Anchor those four to a structural property
   101	# that requires the Taylor lift to be linear in each primitive slip:
   102	for _slip in (Qx, Sx, Hx, Dx, Px, Gx):
   103	    for _name, _expr in (("z2d", z2d), ("z4d", z4d), ("n2d", n2d), ("n4d", n4d)):
   104	        assert_zero(f"{_name} is linear in {_slip}", sp.diff(_expr, _slip, 2))
   105	
   106	# NOTE: The next five independence checks are construction-level, not physical:
   107	# Xi (line 67), K1 (line 68), H_even (line 69) are *defined* without any
   108	# dependence on the listed primitive slips. To exercise the factorization
   109	# claim non-trivially, we below also re-derive the bottlenecks from a
   110	# naive bundle pull-back that contains all six slips, and assert that the
   111	# cancellation to the three-/four-slip form is an algebraic identity.
   112	for sym in (Sx, Hx, Gx):
   113	    assert_zero(f"Xi_load independence from {sym}", sp.diff(Xi, sym))
   114	for sym in (Px, Gx):
   115	    assert_zero(f"K1 independence from {sym}", sp.diff(K1, sym))
   116	    assert_zero(f"H_even independence from {sym}", sp.diff(He, sym))
   117	# Bundle pull-back consistency: rebuild Xi, K1, H_even from z0d/z2d/z4d/n0d
   118	# (which DO contain all six primed slips before simplification), and assert
   119	# the rebuilt forms equal the directly-substituted Xi, K1, H_even.
   120	Xi_bundle = sp.simplify(2*Px/P - 2*Dx/Delta + Qx/(D0*Delta) - Q*Dx/(D0*Delta**2))
   121	assert_zero("Xi bundle pull-back consistency", Xi - Xi_bundle)
   122	K1_bundle = sp.simplify(-(z2d + z0d/sp.Integer(9)))
   123	assert_zero("K1 bundle pull-back consistency", K1 - K1_bundle)
   124	He_bundle = sp.simplify(-(z4d) + sp.Rational(2,3)*z2d - z0d/sp.Integer(27))
   125	assert_zero("H_even bundle pull-back consistency", He - He_bundle)
   126	assert_zero("d Xi_load / d Pprime", coef_Xi_Px - 2/P)
   127	assert_zero("d deltaP2 / d G_W prime", coef_dP2_Gx + 2*P/(D0*Delta**2))
   128	assert_nonzero("deltaP4 should depend on G_W prime", coef_dP4_Gx)
   129	assert_nonzero("source/denominator sieve determinant", qd_matrix.det())
   130	assert_nonzero("spectral sieve determinant", sh_matrix.det())
   131	assert_zero("compensation Hport denominator", Hx_den - 9*Delta**2*(Delta*Hport - Q*S2))
   132	assert_zero("compensation S2 denominator", Sx_den - 9*Delta*(Delta*Hport - Q*S2))
   133	assert_nonzero("mutated compensation denominator should fail", Hx_den - 9*Delta**2*(Delta*Hport + Q*S2))
   134	
   135	if qd_only != [{Qx: 0, Dx: 0}]:
   136	    raise AssertionError(f"Unexpected pure source/denominator solve: {qd_only}")
   137	if sh_only != [{Sx: 0, Hx: 0}]:
   138	    raise AssertionError(f"Unexpected pure spectral solve: {sh_only}")
   139	# Note: the next two assertions are tautological by sp.solve's contract
   140	# (comp_surface is defined as the solve output), kept here for visual
   141	# symmetry with the explicit denominator factorization assertions above.
   142	assert_zero("compensation K1", K1.subs(comp_surface))
   143	assert_zero("compensation H_even", He.subs(comp_surface))
   144	Z2_slot = (Q*S2 - Hport*Delta)/Delta**2
   145	assert_zero("compensation Hport denominator equals -9 Delta^4 Z2", Hx_den - (-9*Delta**4*Z2_slot))
   146	assert_zero("compensation S2 denominator equals -9 Delta^3 Z2", Sx_den - (-9*Delta**3*Z2_slot))
   147	
   148	def p(expr):
   149	    return sp.factor(sp.simplify(expr))
   150	
   151	print("="*96)
   152	print("1) Mouth-local Taylor ansatz")
   153	print("="*96)
   154	print("Take a normalized one-sided mouth kernel w(u), u >= 0, with first moment mu1 = ∫ u w(u) du.")
   155	print("Then for any primitive one-port quantity X(s),")
   156	print("  X_proj(ell) = X(0) + ell * mu1 * X'(0) + O(ell^2).")
   157	print("So")
   158	print("  q1 = mu1*Q'(0),   s1 = mu1*S2'(0),   h1 = mu1*H_port'(0),")
   159	print("  d1 = mu1*Delta'(0), p1 = mu1*P'(0),  g1 = mu1*G_W'(0).")
   160	print("For a symmetric interior kernel, mu1 = 0 and the whole first layer vanishes.\n")
   161	
   162	print("="*96)
   163	print("2) Primitive-to-bundle mouth-derivative map (divide out mu1)")
   164	print("="*96)
   165	print("z0/mu1 =")
   166	print(" ", p(z0d))
   167	print("z2/mu1 =")
   168	print(" ", p(z2d))
   169	print("z4/mu1 =")
   170	print(" ", p(z4d))
   171	print("n0/mu1 =")
   172	print(" ", p(n0d))
   173	print("n2/mu1 =")
   174	print(" ", p(n2d))
   175	print("n4/mu1 =")
   176	print(" ", p(n4d))

   180	print("3) Direct projected-Maxwell contribution to the 5PN bottlenecks")

   238	print("  Delta*H_port - Q*S2.")
   239	print("This is exactly the primitive combination behind the conservative Z2 slot:")
   240	print("  Z2 = (Q*S2 - H_port*Delta)/Delta^2.")
   241	print("So the even-gate compensation becomes singular on the surface Delta*H_port = Q*S2")
   242	print("(equivalently Z2 = 0).")
   243	print()
   244	
   245	print("="*96)
   246	print("7) Readout")
   247	print("="*96)
   248	print("The mouth-local projected-Maxwell packet factorizes into three jobs:")
   249	print("  (i)  (Q', Delta', S2', H_port') repair the conservative even gates K1 and H_even,")
   250	print("  (ii) P' directly tunes the weak-axisymmetric prefactor slope Xi_load,")
   251	print("  (iii) G_W' first tunes the constant-prefactor transport through delta P2 and delta P4.")
   252	print()
   253	print("This is precisely the kind of multi-channel structure you would expect from a real")
   254	print("near-throat missing piece, and it is much sharper than a single scalar correction.")
   255	print("STATUS: PASS")

     1	\section[Stage 015]{Stage 015: Parent throat action master}
     2	\label{stage:015}
     3	
     4	\stagefield{Part / anchor}{Part I; primary anchor \resultanchor{MTDC-T4}.}
     5	\claimstatus{This stage is \StatusExact{} for the displayed algebraic identities inside the declared parent/projection data, with \StatusReduced{} or \StatusExactClosure{} inherited where the local text invokes the matched reduction, mouth-kernel closure, parent-action packet, or one-port normal form.}

     7	\paragraph{Source and audit.}
     8	This stage corresponds to
     9	\StageFile{scripts/moving_throat_pde_stage015_parent_throat_action_master_sympy_audit.py}.
    10	It begins the parent-action continuation of the projected EM bundle.
    11	
    12	\paragraph{Promotion.}
    13	The throat is promoted to an autonomous graph field \(R(\Omega,w,t)\) with
    14	\begin{equation}
    15	\label{eq:stage008-parent-action}
    16	S_\Sigma[R]=\int dt\,dw\,d\Omega\;\mathcal L_\Sigma,
    17	\end{equation}
    18	\begin{equation}
    19	\label{eq:stage015-parent-density}
    20	\mathcal L_\Sigma
    21	=\frac12\mu_\Sigma R_t^2
    22	-\frac12T_{w,\Sigma}R_w^2
    23	-\frac12T_{\Omega,\Sigma}|\nabla_\Omega R|^2
    24	-U_\Sigma .
    25	\end{equation}
    26	The total parent-level model is
    27	\begin{equation}
    28	\label{eq:stage015-total-action}
    29	S_{\rm total}=S_\psi[\psi,A;\Sigma]+S_{\rm EM}[A]+S_\Sigma[R].
    30	\end{equation}
    31	
    32	\paragraph{Quadratic limit.}
    33	The quadratic stiffness around \(R_0(w)\) is
    34	\begin{equation}
    35	\label{eq:stage015-keta}
    36	K_\eta=
    37	U_{\Sigma,RR}(R_0,w)
    38	-\partial_w\!\bigl(T_{w,\Sigma,R}(R_0,w)R_0'\bigr)
    39	+\frac12T_{w,\Sigma,RR}(R_0,w)(R_0')^2.
    40	\end{equation}
    41	The audit carries the boundary densities explicitly and tests both zero and
    42	nonzero boundary-discharge probes.
    43	
    44	\paragraph{Output.}
    45	Stage~015 exports the parent throat action promotion and the exact
    46	quadratic recovery formula \eqref{eq:stage015-keta}.

     9	def assert_zero(label: str, expr: sp.Expr) -> None:
    10	    residue = sp.factor(sp.together(sp.simplify(expr)))
    11	    if residue != 0:
    12	        raise AssertionError(f"{label} failed: {sp.sstr(residue)}")
    13	
    14	
    15	def assert_nonzero(label: str, expr: sp.Expr) -> None:
    16	    value = sp.factor(sp.together(sp.simplify(expr)))
    17	    if value == 0:
    18	        raise AssertionError(f"{label} unexpectedly vanished")
    19	
    20	
    21	def boundary_value(expr: sp.Expr, var: sp.Symbol) -> sp.Expr:
    22	    return sp.simplify(sp.limit(expr, var, sp.oo) - sp.limit(expr, var, -sp.oo))
    23	
    24	
    25	def real_y20_square_ratio(m: int) -> sp.Expr:
    26	    base = sp.simplify(gaunt(2, 2, 2, 0, 0, 0))
    27	    if m != 0:
    28	        same_sign = sp.simplify(gaunt(2, 2, 2, 0, m, m))
    29	        if same_sign != 0:
    30	            raise AssertionError(f"Real-harmonic same-sign cross term should vanish for m={m}: {same_sign}")
    31	    return sp.simplify((sp.Integer(-1) ** m) * gaunt(2, 2, 2, 0, m, -m) / base)

    33	
    34	def grouped_trace_anomaly(x20: sp.Expr, x21: sp.Expr, x22: sp.Expr) -> tuple[sp.Expr, sp.Expr, sp.Expr]:
    35	    xbar = sp.simplify((x20 + 2 * x21 + 2 * x22) / 5)
    36	    ax = sp.simplify((2 * x20 - x21 - x22) / 10)
    37	    bx = sp.simplify((x21 - x22) / 2)
    38	    return xbar, ax, bx

    41	def main() -> None:
    42	    w_ibp = sp.symbols("w_ibp", real=True)
    43	    Aw = sp.Function("A")(w_ibp)
    44	    etaw = sp.Function("eta")(w_ibp)
    45	    quad_boundary_density = -Aw * etaw**2 / 2
    46	    quad_bulk_density = sp.diff(Aw, w_ibp) * etaw**2 / 2
    47	    assert_zero(
    48	        "generic quadratic IBP identity",
    49	        -Aw * etaw * sp.diff(etaw, w_ibp) - (sp.diff(quad_boundary_density, w_ibp) + quad_bulk_density),
    50	    )
    51	    assert_nonzero(
    52	        "mutated quadratic IBP sign should fail",
    53	        -Aw * etaw * sp.diff(etaw, w_ibp) - (sp.diff(-quad_boundary_density, w_ibp) + quad_bulk_density),
    54	    )
    55	    A_concrete = sp.exp(-w_ibp**2)
    56	    eta_concrete = sp.exp(-w_ibp**2 / 2)
    57	    nonzero_boundary_probe = boundary_value(sp.atan(w_ibp), w_ibp)
    58	    assert_nonzero("boundary operator detects nonzero endpoint discharge", nonzero_boundary_probe)
    59	    quad_boundary_concrete = boundary_value((-A_concrete * eta_concrete**2 / 2), w_ibp)

    61	        -A_concrete * eta_concrete * sp.diff(eta_concrete, w_ibp),

    63	    )

    65	        sp.diff(A_concrete, w_ibp) * eta_concrete**2 / 2,

    67	    )

    69	    assert_zero(
    70	        "concrete quadratic IBP with boundary",
    71	        quad_cross_concrete - (quad_boundary_concrete + quad_bulk_concrete),
    72	    )
    73

    75	    eta, eta_t, eta_w, grad2 = sp.symbols("eta eta_t eta_w grad2")
    76	    R0p = sp.symbols("R0p")
    77	    mu0, Tw0, TO0, U0 = sp.symbols("mu0 Tw0 TO0 U0")

   100	    ) * eta**2 / 2

    83	Tw = Tw0 + eps*TwR0*eta + eps^2*TwRR0*eta^2/2;
    84	U = U0 + eps*UR0*eta + eps^2*URR0*eta^2/2;
    85	Rt = eps*etat;
    86	Rw = R0p + eps*etaw;
    87	lagrangian = mu0*Rt^2/2 - Tw*Rw^2/2 - TO0*eps^2*grad2/2 - U;
    88	L2raw = Coefficient[Series[lagrangian, {eps, 0, 2}] // Normal, eps, 2];
    89	crossCoeff = D[D[L2raw, eta], etaw];
    90	expectZero["M3 K_eta raw eta etaw cross coefficient", crossCoeff + TwR0*R0p];
    91	
    92	effectiveMass = URR0 - dTwRR0p + TwRR0*R0p^2/2;
    93	canonicalL2 =
    94	  mu0*etat^2/2 - Tw0*etaw^2/2 - TO0*grad2/2 - effectiveMass*eta^2/2;
    95	L2afterIBP = Expand[L2raw - (-TwR0*R0p*eta*etaw) + dTwRR0p*eta^2/2];
    96	expectZero["M3 K_eta canonical quadratic form", L2afterIBP - canonicalL2];
    97	
    98	effectiveMassMutated = URR0 + dTwRR0p + TwRR0*R0p^2/2;
    99	canonicalL2Mutated =
   100	  mu0*etat^2/2 - Tw0*etaw^2/2 - TO0*grad2/2 -
   101	    effectiveMassMutated*eta^2/2;
   102	expectNonzero["M3 K_eta dTwRR0p sign mutation", L2afterIBP - canonicalL2Mutated];
   103	
   104	D01full = dK - b01 - z01;
   105	D21full = -(dM + b21 + z21);
   106	D41full = -(b41 + z41);
   107	K1full = D21full + D01full/9;
   108	Hevenfull = D41full - (2/3)*D21full - D01full/27;
   109	wallSpec = {b01 -> 0, b21 -> 0, b41 -> 0, z01 -> 0, z21 -> 0, z41 -> 0};
   110	K1wall = Expand[K1full /. wallSpec];
   111	Hevenwall = Expand[Hevenfull /. wallSpec];
   112	expectZero["M4 wall-only K1 specialization", K1wall - (-dM + dK/9)];
   113	expectZero["M4 wall-only H_even specialization", Hevenwall - ((2/3)*dM - dK/27)];
   114	
   115	betaConcrete = Exp[-w^2];
   116	deltaMu = Exp[-w^2];
   117	deltaTw = Exp[-w^2];
   118	deltaTO = Exp[-w^2];
   119	deltaKeta = Exp[-w^2];
   120	dMoverlap = Integrate[deltaMu*betaConcrete^2, {w, -Infinity, Infinity}];
   121	dKoverlap =
   122	  Integrate[
   123	    deltaTw*D[betaConcrete, w]^2 + (deltaKeta + 6*deltaTO)*betaConcrete^2,
   124	    {w, -Infinity, Infinity}
   125	  ];
   126	expectZero["M5 Gaussian dM overlap closed form", dMoverlap - Sqrt[Pi/3]];
   127	expectZero["M5 Gaussian dK overlap closed form", dKoverlap - 23*Sqrt[Pi]/(3*Sqrt[3])];
   128	
   129	K1wallNum = K1wall /. {dK -> dKoverlap, dM -> dMoverlap};
   130	HevenwallNum = Hevenwall /. {dK -> dKoverlap, dM -> dMoverlap};
   131	expectZero[
   132	  "M5 wall-only K1 from concrete Gaussian overlap integrals",
   133	  K1wallNum - (-dMoverlap + dKoverlap/9)
   134	];
   135	expectZero[
   136	  "M5 wall-only H_even from concrete Gaussian overlap integrals",
   137	  HevenwallNum - ((2/3)*dMoverlap - dKoverlap/27)
   138	];
   139	dKoverlapMutated =
   140	  Integrate[
   141	    deltaTw*D[betaConcrete, w]^2 + (deltaKeta + 5*deltaTO)*betaConcrete^2,
   142	    {w, -Infinity, Infinity}
   143	  ];
   144	K1wallMutated = K1wall /. {dK -> dKoverlapMutated, dM -> dMoverlap};
   145	expectNonzero[
   146	  "M5 wall-only K1 detects mutated 6 deltaTO coefficient",
   147	  K1wallMutated - (-dMoverlap + dKoverlap/9)
   148	];
   149	
   150	gateMatrix = {{D[K1wall, dK], D[K1wall, dM]}, {D[Hevenwall, dK], D[Hevenwall, dM]}};
   151	expectZero["M6 wall-only Jacobian determinant", Det[gateMatrix] - 1/27];
   152	
   153	K1wallParam = -dM + gateCoeff*dK;
   154	gateMatrixParam = {
   155	  {D[K1wallParam, dK], D[K1wallParam, dM]},
   156	  {D[Hevenwall, dK], D[Hevenwall, dM]}
   157	};
   158	wallDetShift =
   159	  FullSimplify[
   160	    (Det[gateMatrixParam] /. gateCoeff -> 1/9 + eps) -
   161	      (Det[gateMatrixParam] /. gateCoeff -> 1/9),
   162	    Assumptions -> $Assumptions
   163	  ];
   164	expectZero["M6 wall determinant perturbation value", wallDetShift - 2*eps/3];
   165	expectNonzero["M6 wall determinant perturbation nonzero guard", wallDetShift];
   166	
   167	wallSolve = Solve[{K1wall == 0, Hevenwall == 0}, {dK, dM}];
   168	expectEqual["M7 wall-only zero solve", wallSolve, {{dK -> 0, dM -> 0}}];
   169	wallSolvePerturbed = Solve[{K1wall + eps == 0, Hevenwall == 0}, {dK, dM}];
   170	expectNonzero["M7 perturbed solve nonzero dK", dK /. First[wallSolvePerturbed]];
   171	expectNonzero["M7 perturbed solve nonzero dM", dM /. First[wallSolvePerturbed]];
   172	
   173	gauntBase = ThreeJSymbol[{2, 0}, {2, 0}, {2, 0}];
   174	realY20Ratio[m_] := (-1)^m*ThreeJSymbol[{2, 0}, {2, m}, {2, -m}]/gauntBase;
   175	expectZero["M8 real-Y20 ratio m=0", realY20Ratio[0] - 1];
   176	expectZero["M8 real-Y20 ratio m=1", realY20Ratio[1] - 1/2];
   177	expectZero["M8 real-Y20 ratio m=2", realY20Ratio[2] + 1];
   178	Do[
   179	  expectZero[
   180	    "M8 same-sign cross term m=" <> ToString[m],
   181	    Quiet[ThreeJSymbol[{2, 0}, {2, m}, {2, m}], ClebschGordan::phy]
   182	  ],
   183	  {m, {1, 2}}
   184	];
   185	
   186	lam20 = 1;
   187	lam21 = 1/2;
   188	lam22 = -1;
   189	x20 = x0 + eps1*lam20;
   190	x21 = x0 + eps1*lam21;
   191	x22 = x0 + eps1*lam22;
   192	xbar = (x20 + 2*x21 + 2*x22)/5;
   193	ax = (2*x20 - x21 - x22)/10;
   194	bx = (x21 - x22)/2;
   195	expectZero["M9 grouped trace", xbar - x0];
   196	expectZero["M9 grouped line b=3a", bx - 3*ax];
   197	
   198	Print["STATUS: PASS"];
   199	Exit[0];

     1	\section[Stage 020]{Stage 020: Parent throat action weak-axisymmetric packet}

     5	\claimstatus{This stage is \StatusExact{} for the displayed algebraic identities inside the declared parent/projection data, with \StatusReduced{} or \StatusExactClosure{} inherited where the local text invokes the matched reduction, mouth-kernel closure, parent-action packet, or one-port normal form.}
     6

     9	\StageFile{scripts/moving_throat_pde_stage020_parent_throat_action_weak_axisym_packet_sympy_audit.py}.

    12	\paragraph{Slope packet.}

    14	\begin{equation}
    15	\label{eq:stage020-wall-slopes}
    16	\delta M_\Sigma=\int dw\,\delta\mu_\eta\,\beta_2^2,\qquad

    18	(\delta K_\eta+6\delta T_\Omega)\beta_2^2\Bigr].

    22	\label{eq:stage020-denominator-slopes}
    23	D_{01}=\delta K_\Sigma-B_{01}-Z_{01},\quad

    26	\end{equation}

    45	\end{equation}

    51	\end{equation}

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	\section[Stage 022]{Stage 022: Grouped Real \(P_2\) Normalization Bridge}
     2	\label{stage:022}
     3	
     4	\stagefield{Part / anchor}{Part I; primary anchors \resultanchor{MTDC-T3} and \resultanchor{MTDC-T4}.}
     5	\claimstatus{The bridge formulas are \StatusExactClosure{} inside the grouped response normalization convention.  The final equality to the universal normalization is a \StatusOpen{} branch-realization test.}
     6	
     7	\stagefield{Purpose}{Stage~022 translates the Stages~004--021 projection-first Maxwell packet, through its matched one-port outgoing normal form, into the grouped real \(P_2\) response language.  It converts conservative operator moments \(D_{An}\) into normalized response moments \(u_n^{(A)}\), tracks the outgoing-transfer prefactor \(P_{An}\), and isolates the exact invariant product that must match the universal quadrupole normalization.}
     8	
     9	\stagefield{Inputs}{The inputs are one conservative grouped-lane operator \(D_A^{\rm cons}(\omega)\), one outgoing transfer factor \(N_A(\omega)\) exported by the matched Stage~021 one-port normal form, the compact outgoing \(l=2\) fingerprint, and the grouped trace/anomaly map.}
    10	
    11	\stagefield{Verification}{SymPy audit: \StageFile{scripts/moving_throat_pde_stage022_grouped_p2_sympy_audit.py}; transcript: \StageFile{scripts/output/moving_throat_pde_stage022_grouped_p2_sympy_audit.txt}.  Mathematica audit: \StageFile{mathematica/moving_throat_pde_stage022_grouped_p2_normalization_bridge_mathematica_audit.wl}; transcript: \StageFile{mathematica/output/moving_throat_pde_stage022_grouped_p2_normalization_bridge_mathematica_audit.txt}.}
    12	
    13	\subsection*{Derivation}
    14	
    15	\paragraph{1. Conservative operator and normalized response.}
    16	For each grouped lane \(A\in\{20,21,22\}\), write
    17	\begin{equation}
    18	\label{eq:app-stage022-d-operator}
    19	D_A^{\rm cons}(\omega)=D_{A0}+D_{A2}\omega^2+D_{A4}\omega^4+O(\omega^6).
    20	\end{equation}
    21	The normalized conservative response is
    22	\begin{equation}
    23	\label{eq:app-stage022-y-response}
    24	Y_A(\omega)=\frac{D_{A0}}{D_A^{\rm cons}(\omega)}
    25	=1+u_2^{(A)}\omega^2+u_4^{(A)}\omega^4+O(\omega^6).
    26	\end{equation}
    27	Expanding the inverse gives
    28	\begin{equation}
    29	\label{eq:app-stage022-u2u4}
    30	\boxed{
    31	u_2^{(A)}=-\frac{D_{A2}}{D_{A0}},
    32	\qquad
    33	u_4^{(A)}=\frac{D_{A2}^2-D_{A0}D_{A4}}{D_{A0}^2}}.
    34	\end{equation}
    35	Here the symbol \(u_n^{(A)}\) is used in the main ledger; the boxed formula records the same coefficients.  The key point is that the normalized response moments are not independent of the operator moments.
    36	
    37	\paragraph{2. Grouped inverse map.}
    38	For any grouped triple \(x_A=(x_{20},x_{21},x_{22})\), define
    39	\begin{equation}
    40	\label{eq:app-stage022-grouped-defs}
    41	\bar x=\frac{x_{20}+2x_{21}+2x_{22}}{5},
    42	\qquad
    43	a_x=\frac{2x_{20}-x_{21}-x_{22}}{10},
    44	\qquad
    45	b_x=\frac{x_{21}-x_{22}}{2}.
    46	\end{equation}
    47	The inverse map is
    48	\begin{equation}
    49	\label{eq:app-stage022-grouped-inverse}
    50	\boxed{
    51	x_{20}=\bar x+4a_x,
    52	\qquad
    53	x_{21}=\bar x-a_x+b_x,
    54	\qquad
    55	x_{22}=\bar x-a_x-b_x}.
    56	\end{equation}
    57	The grouped isotropy gate is
    58	\begin{equation}
    59	\label{eq:app-stage022-isotropy-gate}
    60	a_2=b_2=a_4=b_4=0
    61	\end{equation}
    62	when applied to \(u_2^{(A)}\) and \(u_4^{(A)}\).
    63	
    64	\paragraph{3. Internal outgoing prefactor.}
    65	Let
    66	\begin{equation}
    67	\label{eq:app-stage022-n-transfer}
    68	N_A(\omega)=N_{A0}+N_{A2}\omega^2+N_{A4}\omega^4+O(\omega^6).
    69	\end{equation}
    70	To first order in the outgoing branch, the normalized response correction is controlled by
    71	\begin{equation}
    72	\label{eq:app-stage022-pref-def}
    73	{\rm Pref}_A(\omega)
    74	=\frac{D_{A0}N_A(\omega)}{\left(D_A^{\rm cons}(\omega)\right)^2}
    75	=P_{A0}+P_{A2}\omega^2+P_{A4}\omega^4+O(\omega^6).
    76	\end{equation}
    77	Direct expansion gives
    78	\begin{align}
    79	\label{eq:app-stage022-pref-coeffs}
    80	\boxed{P_{A0}}&=\boxed{\frac{N_{A0}}{D_{A0}}},\\
    81	\boxed{P_{A2}}&=\boxed{\frac{D_{A0}N_{A2}-2D_{A2}N_{A0}}{D_{A0}^2}},\\
    82	\boxed{P_{A4}}&=\boxed{\frac{D_{A0}^2N_{A4}-2D_{A0}(D_{A2}N_{A2}+D_{A4}N_{A0})+3D_{A2}^2N_{A0}}{D_{A0}^3}}.
    83	\end{align}
    84	These are the algebraic bridge from conservative internal data to outgoing-branch prefactor data.
    85	
    86	\paragraph{4. Multiplication by the compact outgoing fingerprint.}
    87	Write the compact outgoing branch as
    88	\begin{equation}
    89	\label{eq:app-stage022-outgoing-compact}
    90	\widehat Y_2^{\rm out}(\omega)
    91	=1+A\omega^2+B\omega^4+iG_5\omega^5+O(\omega^6),
    92	\end{equation}
    93	with
    94	\begin{equation}
    95	\label{eq:app-stage022-abg}
    96	A=\frac{a^2}{9c_s^2},
    97	\qquad
    98	B=\frac{4a^4}{81c_s^4},
    99	\qquad
   100	G_5=\frac{a^5}{27c_s^5}.
   101	\end{equation}
   102	Then
   103	\begin{equation}
   104	\delta Y_A^{\rm out}(\omega)={\rm Pref}_A(\omega)\widehat Y_2^{\rm out}(\omega)
   105	=K_{A0}+K_{A2}\omega^2+K_{A4}\omega^4+i\Gamma_5^{(A)}\omega^5+\cdots,
   106	\end{equation}
   107	where
   108	\begin{align}
   109	\label{eq:app-stage022-k-gamma}
   110	K_{A0}&=P_{A0},\\
   111	K_{A2}&=P_{A2}+AP_{A0},\\
   112	K_{A4}&=P_{A4}+AP_{A2}+BP_{A0},\\
   113	\Gamma_5^{(A)}&=G_5P_{A0}.
   114	\end{align}
   115	Thus only the static prefactor \(P_{A0}\) enters the leading odd \(i\omega^5\) coefficient.
   116	
   117	\paragraph{5. Universal normalization product.}
   118	The GR quadrupole target is
   119	\begin{equation}
   120	\label{eq:app-stage022-gamma-gr}
   121	\gamma_{\rm GR}=\frac{2G}{5c^5}.
   122	\end{equation}
   123	Including the source-map factor \(\widehat m_0\), the invariant normalization condition is
   124	\begin{equation}
   125	\label{eq:app-stage022-invariant-normalization}
   126	\boxed{
   127	\widehat m_0^{\,2}P_0\frac{a^5}{27c_s^5}=\frac{2G}{5c^5}},
   128	\end{equation}
   129	or equivalently
   130	\begin{equation}
   131	\label{eq:app-stage022-p0-target}
   132	\boxed{
   133	\widehat m_0^{\,2}P_0=\frac{54Gc_s^5}{5a^5c^5}}.
   134	\end{equation}
   135	On the leading natural source-map branch \(\widehat m_0=1+O(a^2/r^2)\), this reduces to a direct target for \(P_0\).
   136	
   137	\stagefield{Output}{Stage~022 outputs the normalized response formulas \eqref{eq:app-stage022-u2u4}, the grouped inverse map \eqref{eq:app-stage022-grouped-inverse}, the prefactor coefficients \eqref{eq:app-stage022-pref-coeffs}, the odd coefficient \eqref{eq:app-stage022-k-gamma}, and the invariant normalization test \eqref{eq:app-stage022-p0-target}.}
   138	
   139	\stagefield{Checks}{\begin{verificationchecklist}
   140	\item The inverse expansion of \(D_{A0}/D_A\) gives \(u_2,u_4\) with the signs shown in \eqref{eq:app-stage022-u2u4}.
   141	\item The grouped inverse map \eqref{eq:app-stage022-grouped-inverse} reproduces \eqref{eq:app-stage022-grouped-defs} exactly.
   142	\item The first odd coefficient depends only on \(P_{A0}\), because \({\rm Pref}_A\) is even through the retained orders and the outgoing odd term first appears at \(\omega^5\).
   143	\item The final equality \eqref{eq:app-stage022-p0-target} is a branch test, not a proof of branch realization.
   144	\end{verificationchecklist}}
   145	
   146	\stagefield{Downstream use}{Stage~023 uses the Stage~022 bridge to define \(P_0=N_0/D_0\) for the full grouped bundle.  The 2.5PN and 4PN bridge sections use \eqref{eq:app-stage022-p0-target} as the shared outgoing-normalization condition.  Later same-charge and weak-axisymmetric stages use the ratio \(P_1/P_0\) as the transported prefactor slope.}

     1	\section[Stage 023]{Stage 023: Full Grouped Bundle, Exact Projectors, and Isotropic Normalization Test}
     2	\label{stage:023}
     3	
     4	\stagefield{Part / anchor}{Part I; primary anchor \resultanchor{MTDC-T4}, preparing \resultanchor{MTDC-T5}.}
     5	\claimstatus{The grouped projectors, coefficient formulas, isotropic reduction, constant-prefactor conditions, and first-order transport laws are \StatusExactClosure{} inside the full reduced grouped-bundle model.  Actual values of the microscopic branch data remain \StatusOpen{}.}
     6	
     7	\stagefield{Purpose}{Stage~023 writes the entire grouped real \(20/21/22\) bundle in one place.  It combines the wall baseline, BdG support moments, projection-first Maxwell/matched mixed conservative moments, and outgoing-transfer moments; introduces exact weighted projectors for the grouped lanes; states the isotropic normalization test in full-bundle variables; and derives the first-order anisotropy transport laws.}
     8	
     9	\stagefield{Inputs}{The inputs are the Stage~003 BdG moments, the Stages~004--021 projected Maxwell-to-\(Z_n,N_n\) packet and matched one-port conservative/transfer moments, the Stage~022 response/prefactor conversion, and the grouped metric \(G_{\rm grp}=\operatorname{diag}(1,2,2)\).}
    10	
    11	\stagefield{Verification}{SymPy audit: \StageFile{scripts/moving_throat_pde_stage023_full_grouped_bundle_sympy_audit.py}; transcript: \StageFile{scripts/output/moving_throat_pde_stage023_full_grouped_bundle_sympy_audit.txt}.  Mathematica audit: \StageFile{mathematica/moving_throat_pde_stage023_full_grouped_bundle_mathematica_audit.wl}; transcript: \StageFile{mathematica/output/moving_throat_pde_stage023_full_grouped_bundle_mathematica_audit.txt}.}
    12	
    13	\subsection*{Derivation}
    14	
    15	\paragraph{1. Weighted grouped projector calculus.}
    16	The grouped triple \((20,21,22)\) carries metric
    17	\begin{equation}
    18	\label{eq:app-stage023-grouped-metric}
    19	G_{\rm grp}=\operatorname{diag}(1,2,2).
    20	\end{equation}
    21	Use the \(G_{\rm grp}\)-orthogonal basis
    22	\begin{equation}
    23	\label{eq:app-stage023-basis}
    24	e_{\bar x}=(1,1,1),
    25	\qquad
    26	e_a=(4,-1,-1),
    27	\qquad
    28	e_b=(0,1,-1).
    29	\end{equation}
    30	The norms are
    31	\begin{equation}
    32	\label{eq:app-stage023-norms}
    33	e_{\bar x}^TG_{\rm grp}e_{\bar x}=5,
    34	\qquad
    35	e_a^TG_{\rm grp}e_a=20,
    36	\qquad
    37	e_b^TG_{\rm grp}e_b=4.
    38	\end{equation}
    39	Thus any grouped vector decomposes as
    40	\begin{equation}
    41	\label{eq:app-stage023-decomp}
    42	x=\bar x\,e_{\bar x}+a_xe_a+b_xe_b,
    43	\end{equation}
    44	with \(\bar x,a_x,b_x\) as in \eqref{eq:app-stage022-grouped-defs}.  The exact projectors are
    45	\begin{equation}
    46	\label{eq:app-stage023-projectors}
    47	\boxed{
    48	P_{\bar x}=\frac15
    49	\begin{pmatrix}
    50	1&2&2\\1&2&2\\1&2&2
    51	\end{pmatrix},
    52	\quad
    53	P_a=\frac1{20}
    54	\begin{pmatrix}
    55	16&-8&-8\\-4&2&2\\-4&2&2
    56	\end{pmatrix},
    57	\quad
    58	P_b=\frac14
    59	\begin{pmatrix}
    60	0&0&0\\0&2&-2\\0&-2&2
    61	\end{pmatrix}}.
    62	\end{equation}
    63	They obey
    64	\begin{equation}
    65	\label{eq:app-stage023-projector-identities}
    66	P_{\bar x}+P_a+P_b=I_3,
    67	\qquad
    68	P_iP_j=\delta_{ij}P_i.
    69	\end{equation}
    70	Grouped isotropy is the condition
    71	\begin{equation}
    72	\label{eq:app-stage023-isotropy-projector}
    73	P_ax=P_bx=0.
    74	\end{equation}
    75	
    76	\paragraph{2. Full coupled lane Lagrangian.}
    77	For each lane \(A\in\{20,21,22\}\), retain a wall amplitude \(q_A\), stable BdG support modes \(X_{A\alpha}\), brane-like gauge coordinates \(U_{A r}\), mixed coordinates \(W_{A r}\), and internal gauge mixing \(R_{A r}\).  The reduced Lagrangian is
    78	\begin{align}
    79	\label{eq:app-stage023-full-lagrangian}
    80	L_A={}&\frac12M_A\dot q_A^2-\frac12K_Aq_A^2
    81	+\sum_\alpha\left[\frac12\dot X_{A\alpha}^2-\frac12\varpi_{A\alpha}^2X_{A\alpha}^2+c_{A\alpha}q_AX_{A\alpha}\right]\\
    82	&+\sum_r\bigl[\frac12\dot U_{A r}^2-\frac12\Omega_{U,A r}^2U_{A r}^2
    83	+\frac12\dot W_{A r}^2-\frac12\Omega_{W,A r}^2W_{A r}^2
    84	+R_{A r}U_{A r}W_{A r}\\
    85	&\qquad\quad
    86	+g_{U,A r}q_AU_{A r}+g_{W,A r}q_AW_{A r}\bigr].
    87	\end{align}
    88	
    89	\paragraph{3. BdG support moments.}
    90	Define
    91	\begin{equation}
    92	\label{eq:app-stage023-b-moments}
    93	\boxed{
    94	B_{A0}=\sum_\alpha\frac{c_{A\alpha}^2}{\varpi_{A\alpha}^2},
    95	\qquad
    96	B_{A2}=\sum_\alpha\frac{c_{A\alpha}^2}{\varpi_{A\alpha}^4},
    97	\qquad
    98	B_{A4}=\sum_\alpha\frac{c_{A\alpha}^2}{\varpi_{A\alpha}^6}}.
    99	\end{equation}
   100	These are the Stage~003 Schur-complement moments collected lane by lane.
   101	
   102	\paragraph{4. Conservative Maxwell/mixed moments.}
   103	For each port \(r\), set
   104	\begin{equation}
   105	\label{eq:app-stage023-delta-s-q-g}
   106	\Delta_{A r}=\Omega_{U,A r}^2\Omega_{W,A r}^2-R_{A r}^2,
   107	\qquad
   108	S_{A r}=\Omega_{U,A r}^2+\Omega_{W,A r}^2,
   109	\end{equation}
   110	\begin{equation}
   111	Q_{A r}=g_{U,A r}^2\Omega_{W,A r}^2
   112	+2g_{U,A r}g_{W,A r}R_{A r}
   113	+g_{W,A r}^2\Omega_{U,A r}^2,
   114	\qquad
   115	G_{A r}=g_{U,A r}^2+g_{W,A r}^2.
   116	\end{equation}
   117	The conservative Maxwell/mixed moments are
   118	\begin{align}
   119	\label{eq:app-stage023-z-moments-port}
   120	Z_{A0}^{(r)}&=\frac{Q_{A r}}{\Delta_{A r}},\\
   121	Z_{A2}^{(r)}&=\frac{Q_{A r}S_{A r}-G_{A r}\Delta_{A r}}{\Delta_{A r}^2},\\
   122	Z_{A4}^{(r)}&=\frac{Q_{A r}(S_{A r}^2-\Delta_{A r})-S_{A r}G_{A r}\Delta_{A r}}{\Delta_{A r}^3},
   123	\end{align}
   124	and
   125	\begin{equation}
   126	\label{eq:app-stage023-z-sum}
   127	Z_{An}=\sum_rZ_{An}^{(r)},
   128	\qquad
   129	n\in\{0,2,4\}.
   130	\end{equation}
   131	
   132	\paragraph{5. Outgoing-transfer moments.}
   133	Define
   134	\begin{equation}
   135	\label{eq:app-stage023-p-ar}
   136	P_{A r}=\Omega_{U,A r}^2g_{W,A r}+R_{A r}g_{U,A r}.
   137	\end{equation}
   138	The outgoing-transfer moments per port are
   139	\begin{align}
   140	\label{eq:app-stage023-n-moments-port}
   141	N_{A0}^{(r)}&=\frac{P_{A r}^2}{\Delta_{A r}^2},\\
   142	N_{A2}^{(r)}&=\frac{2P_{A r}\bigl(P_{A r}S_{A r}-\Delta_{A r}g_{W,A r}\bigr)}{\Delta_{A r}^3},\\
   143	N_{A4}^{(r)}&=\frac{\Delta_{A r}^2g_{W,A r}^2-2\Delta_{A r}P_{A r}^2-4\Delta_{A r}P_{A r}S_{A r}g_{W,A r}+3P_{A r}^2S_{A r}^2}{\Delta_{A r}^4}.
   144	\end{align}
   145	Summing over outgoing ports gives
   146	\begin{equation}
   147	\label{eq:app-stage023-n-sum}
   148	N_{An}=\sum_rN_{An}^{(r)},
   149	\qquad
   150	n\in\{0,2,4\}.
   151	\end{equation}
   152	
   153	\paragraph{6. Total conservative grouped operator.}
   154	The full conservative grouped-lane operator is
   155	\begin{equation}
   156	\label{eq:app-stage023-d-total}
   157	D_A^{\rm cons}(\omega)=D_{A0}+D_{A2}\omega^2+D_{A4}\omega^4+O(\omega^6),
   158	\end{equation}
   159	with
   160	\begin{equation}

exec
/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba paper/stages/stage_023.tex | sed -n '160,340p'" in /var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger
 succeeded in 0ms:
   160	\begin{equation}
   161	\label{eq:app-stage023-d-coeffs}
   162	\boxed{
   163	D_{A0}=K_A-B_{A0}-Z_{A0},
   164	\qquad
   165	D_{A2}=-\bigl(M_A+B_{A2}+Z_{A2}\bigr),
   166	\qquad
   167	D_{A4}=-\bigl(B_{A4}+Z_{A4}\bigr)}.
   168	\end{equation}
   169	This is the full Part-I coefficient ledger.
   170	
   171	\paragraph{7. Microscopic anisotropy ledger.}
   172	Apply the grouped decomposition \eqref{eq:app-stage023-decomp} to every microscopic family \(K_A,M_A,B_{An},Z_{An},N_{An}\).  The static conservative defects obey
   173	\begin{equation}
   174	\label{eq:app-stage023-d0-defects}
   175	\bar D_0=\bar K-\bar B_0-\bar Z_0,
   176	\qquad
   177	a_{D,0}=a_K-a_{B,0}-a_{Z,0},
   178	\qquad
   179	b_{D,0}=b_K-b_{B,0}-b_{Z,0}.
   180	\end{equation}
   181	Similarly,
   182	\begin{equation}
   183	\label{eq:app-stage023-d2-defects}
   184	\bar D_2=-(\bar M+\bar B_2+\bar Z_2),
   185	\qquad
   186	a_{D,2}=-(a_M+a_{B,2}+a_{Z,2}),
   187	\qquad
   188	b_{D,2}=-(b_M+b_{B,2}+b_{Z,2}),
   189	\end{equation}
   190	and
   191	\begin{equation}
   192	\label{eq:app-stage023-d4-defects}
   193	\bar D_4=-(\bar B_4+\bar Z_4),
   194	\qquad
   195	a_{D,4}=-(a_{B,4}+a_{Z,4}),
   196	\qquad
   197	b_{D,4}=-(b_{B,4}+b_{Z,4}).
   198	\end{equation}
   199	Thus grouped anisotropy can enter only through the wall baseline, the BdG support bundle, or the Maxwell/mixed conservative and outgoing-transfer bundles.
   200	
   201	\paragraph{8. Isotropic branch and normalization ratio.}
   202	On the isotropic branch,
   203	\begin{equation}
   204	D_{20,n}=D_{21,n}=D_{22,n}=D_n,
   205	\qquad
   206	N_{20,n}=N_{21,n}=N_{22,n}=N_n.
   207	\end{equation}
   208	Stage~022 then gives
   209	\begin{equation}
   210	\label{eq:app-stage023-isotropic-response}
   211	u_2=-\frac{D_2}{D_0},
   212	\qquad
   213	u_4=\frac{D_2^2-D_0D_4}{D_0^2},
   214	\end{equation}
   215	and
   216	\begin{equation}
   217	\label{eq:app-stage023-isotropic-pref}
   218	P_0=\frac{N_0}{D_0},
   219	\qquad
   220	P_2=\frac{D_0N_2-2D_2N_0}{D_0^2},
   221	\end{equation}
   222	\begin{equation}
   223	\label{eq:app-stage023-isotropic-p4}
   224	P_4=\frac{D_0^2N_4-2D_0(D_2N_2+D_4N_0)+3D_2^2N_0}{D_0^3}.
   225	\end{equation}
   226	The isotropic normalization test is
   227	\begin{equation}
   228	\label{eq:app-stage023-normalization-test}
   229	\boxed{
   230	\widehat m_0^{\,2}\frac{N_0}{K-B_0-Z_0}
   231	=\frac{54Gc_s^5}{5a^5c^5}}.
   232	\end{equation}
   233	The denominator is the static full-bundle stiffness
   234	\begin{equation}
   235	D_0=K-B_0-Z_0.
   236	\end{equation}
   237	
   238	\paragraph{9. Constant-prefactor branch.}
   239	The constant-prefactor branch through the retained orders is
   240	\begin{equation}
   241	P_2=0,
   242	\qquad
   243	P_4=0.
   244	\end{equation}
   245	Using \eqref{eq:app-stage023-isotropic-pref}--\eqref{eq:app-stage023-isotropic-p4}, this is equivalent to
   246	\begin{equation}
   247	\label{eq:app-stage023-constant-prefactor-conditions}
   248	\boxed{
   249	N_2=\frac{2D_2N_0}{D_0},
   250	\qquad
   251	N_4=\frac{2D_0(D_2N_2+D_4N_0)-3D_2^2N_0}{D_0^2}}.
   252	\end{equation}
   253	So constant outgoing prefactor does not require \(N_2=N_4=0\); it requires precise correlation between transfer and conservative moments.
   254	
   255	\paragraph{10. First-order anisotropy transport.}
   256	Let
   257	\begin{equation}
   258	D_{An}=D_n+\delta D_{An},
   259	\qquad
   260	N_{An}=N_n+\delta N_{An},
   261	\end{equation}
   262	near the isotropic branch.  From \(u_2^{(A)}=-D_{A2}/D_{A0}\),
   263	\begin{equation}
   264	\label{eq:app-stage023-du2}
   265	\delta u_2^{(A)}=-\frac{\delta D_{A2}+u_2\delta D_{A0}}{D_0}.
   266	\end{equation}
   267	Therefore
   268	\begin{equation}
   269	\label{eq:app-stage023-u-anisotropy}
   270	\boxed{
   271	a_{u,2}=-\frac{a_{D,2}+u_2a_{D,0}}{D_0},
   272	\qquad
   273	b_{u,2}=-\frac{b_{D,2}+u_2b_{D,0}}{D_0}}.
   274	\end{equation}
   275	Similarly, from \(P_0^{(A)}=N_{A0}/D_{A0}\),
   276	\begin{equation}
   277	\label{eq:app-stage023-dp0}
   278	\delta P_0^{(A)}=\frac{\delta N_{A0}-P_0\delta D_{A0}}{D_0},
   279	\end{equation}
   280	so
   281	\begin{equation}
   282	\label{eq:app-stage023-p-anisotropy}
   283	\boxed{
   284	a_{P,0}=\frac{a_{N,0}-P_0a_{D,0}}{D_0},
   285	\qquad
   286	b_{P,0}=\frac{b_{N,0}-P_0b_{D,0}}{D_0}}.
   287	\end{equation}
   288	Thus outgoing-prefactor anisotropy can arise only from direct anisotropy in \(N_{A0}\) or conservative anisotropy in \(D_{A0}\) reweighted by \(P_0\).
   289	
   290	\stagefield{Output}{Stage~023 outputs the projectors \eqref{eq:app-stage023-projectors}, the full coupled coefficient formulas \eqref{eq:app-stage023-b-moments}, \eqref{eq:app-stage023-z-moments-port}, \eqref{eq:app-stage023-n-moments-port}, and \eqref{eq:app-stage023-d-coeffs}, the isotropic normalization ratio \eqref{eq:app-stage023-normalization-test}, the constant-prefactor conditions \eqref{eq:app-stage023-constant-prefactor-conditions}, and the anisotropy transport laws \eqref{eq:app-stage023-u-anisotropy} and \eqref{eq:app-stage023-p-anisotropy}.}
   291	
   292	\stagefield{Checks}{\begin{verificationchecklist}
   293	\item Direct multiplication verifies \(P_iP_j=\delta_{ij}P_i\) and \(P_{\bar x}+P_a+P_b=I_3\).
   294	\item The BdG, Maxwell/mixed, and outgoing-transfer coefficient formulas reduce to the Stage~003 and Stage~021 one-lane formulas when a single mode/port is retained.
   295	\item The isotropic limit sets all grouped defects to zero and collapses \((20,21,22)\) to one common \(D_n,N_n\) packet.
   296	\item First-order quotient differentiation gives the transport laws \eqref{eq:app-stage023-u-anisotropy} and \eqref{eq:app-stage023-p-anisotropy}.
   297	\item The final normalization equation is a stability-sensitive branch test because \(D_0=K-B_0-Z_0\) must remain positive on the stable side.
   298	\end{verificationchecklist}}
   299	
   300	\stagefield{Downstream use}{Stage~024 begins the explicit overlap-integral and \(O(3)\) isotropy theorem using the Stage~023 coefficient ledger.  Later conservative and retarded stages repeatedly reuse \(D_0,D_2,D_4\), \(N_0,N_2,N_4\), \(P_0,P_2,P_4\), the grouped projectors, and the anisotropy transport laws.  The weak-axisymmetric orbit and same-charge sections eventually reinterpret \(P_1/P_0\) as the transported outgoing-prefactor slope.}

     3	import sympy as sp
     4	from sympy.physics.wigner import gaunt

     6	
     7	def assert_zero(label: str, expr: sp.Expr) -> None:
     8	    residue = sp.factor(sp.together(sp.simplify(expr)))
     9	    if residue != 0:
    10	        raise AssertionError(f"{label} failed: {sp.sstr(residue)}")
    11	
    12	
    13	def assert_nonzero(label: str, expr: sp.Expr) -> None:
    14	    residue = sp.factor(sp.together(sp.simplify(expr)))
    15	    if residue == 0:
    16	        raise AssertionError(f"{label} unexpectedly vanished")
    17	
    18	
    19	def real_y20_square_ratio(m: int) -> sp.Expr:
    20	    base = sp.simplify(gaunt(2, 2, 2, 0, 0, 0))
    21	    if m != 0:
    22	        same_sign = sp.simplify(gaunt(2, 2, 2, 0, m, m))
    23	        if same_sign != 0:
    24	            raise AssertionError(f"Real-harmonic same-sign cross term should vanish for m={m}: {same_sign}")
    25	    return sp.simplify((sp.Integer(-1) ** m) * gaunt(2, 2, 2, 0, m, -m) / base)
    26	
    27	
    28	def grouped_trace_anomaly(x20: sp.Expr, x21: sp.Expr, x22: sp.Expr) -> tuple[sp.Expr, sp.Expr, sp.Expr]:
    29	    xbar = sp.simplify((x20 + 2*x21 + 2*x22) / 5)
    30	    ax = sp.simplify((2*x20 - x21 - x22) / 10)
    31	    bx = sp.simplify((x21 - x22) / 2)
    32	    return xbar, ax, bx
    33

    35	def main() -> None:
    36	    eps = sp.symbols('eps', real=True)
    37	    M1, K1w = sp.symbols('M1 K1w', real=True)
    38	    D0, N0 = sp.symbols('D0 N0', positive=True)
    39	
    40	    lam20 = real_y20_square_ratio(0)
    41	    lam21 = real_y20_square_ratio(1)
    42	    lam22 = real_y20_square_ratio(2)
    43	    assert_zero('Y20 overlap lane 20', lam20 - 1)
    44	    assert_zero('Y20 overlap lane 21', lam21 - sp.Rational(1, 2))
    45	    assert_zero('Y20 overlap lane 22', lam22 + 1)
    46	
    47	    dM20 = eps * lam20 * M1
    48	    dM21 = eps * lam21 * M1
    49	    dM22 = eps * lam22 * M1
    50	
    51	    dK20 = eps * lam20 * K1w
    52	    dK21 = eps * lam21 * K1w
    53	    dK22 = eps * lam22 * K1w
    54	
    55	    Mbar, aM, bM = grouped_trace_anomaly(dM20, dM21, dM22)
    56	    Kbar, aK, bK = grouped_trace_anomaly(dK20, dK21, dK22)
    57	    assert_zero('wall inertia trace', Mbar)
    58	    assert_zero('wall inertia b=3a', bM - 3*aM)
    59	    assert_zero('wall stiffness trace', Kbar)
    60	    assert_zero('wall stiffness b=3a', bK - 3*aK)
    61	
    62	    # Wall-only contributions to the Stage-5 weak-axisymmetric gates.
    63	    D01_20, D01_21, D01_22 = dK20, dK21, dK22
    64	    D21_20, D21_21, D21_22 = -dM20, -dM21, -dM22
    65	    D41_20, D41_21, D41_22 = sp.Integer(0), sp.Integer(0), sp.Integer(0)
    66	
    67	    dKsym, dMsym = sp.symbols('dKsym dMsym', real=True)
    68	    B01, B21, B41 = sp.symbols('B01 B21 B41')
    69	    Z01, Z21, Z41 = sp.symbols('Z01 Z21 Z41')
    70	    D01_full = dKsym - B01 - Z01
    71	    D21_full = -(dMsym + B21 + Z21)
    72	    D41_full = -(B41 + Z41)
    73	    K1_full = sp.expand(D21_full + D01_full / 9)
    74	    Hev_full = sp.expand(D41_full - sp.Rational(2, 3) * D21_full - D01_full / 27)
    75	    wall_only = {B01: 0, B21: 0, B41: 0, Z01: 0, Z21: 0, Z41: 0}
    76	    K1_wall = sp.expand(K1_full.subs(wall_only))
    77	    Hev_wall = sp.expand(Hev_full.subs(wall_only))
    78	
    79	    K1_gate_20 = sp.simplify(D21_20 + D01_20 / 9)
    80	    K1_gate_21 = sp.simplify(D21_21 + D01_21 / 9)
    81	    K1_gate_22 = sp.simplify(D21_22 + D01_22 / 9)
    82	
    83	    Hev_20 = sp.simplify(D41_20 - sp.Rational(2, 3) * D21_20 - D01_20 / 27)
    84	    Hev_21 = sp.simplify(D41_21 - sp.Rational(2, 3) * D21_21 - D01_21 / 27)
    85	    Hev_22 = sp.simplify(D41_22 - sp.Rational(2, 3) * D21_22 - D01_22 / 27)
    86	
    87	    # Cross-check: the generic lane formulas K1_wall = -M1 + K1w/9 and
    88	    # Hev_wall = 2 M1 / 3 - K1w / 27 must reproduce the three explicit lane
    89	    # gates K1_gate_2m and Hev_2m via X_A = eps * lambda_A * X1.
    90	    generic_K1 = -M1 + K1w / sp.Integer(9)
    91	    generic_Hev = sp.Rational(2, 3) * M1 - K1w / sp.Integer(27)
    92	    assert_zero("generic K1 vs lane 20", K1_gate_20 - eps * lam20 * generic_K1)
    93	    assert_zero("generic K1 vs lane 21", K1_gate_21 - eps * lam21 * generic_K1)
    94	    assert_zero("generic K1 vs lane 22", K1_gate_22 - eps * lam22 * generic_K1)
    95	    assert_zero("generic Hev vs lane 20", Hev_20 - eps * lam20 * generic_Hev)
    96	    assert_zero("generic Hev vs lane 21", Hev_21 - eps * lam21 * generic_Hev)
    97	    assert_zero("generic Hev vs lane 22", Hev_22 - eps * lam22 * generic_Hev)
    98	
    99	    # Solve the wall-only even gates.
   100	    wall_matrix = sp.Matrix([
   101	        [sp.diff(K1_wall, dKsym), sp.diff(K1_wall, dMsym)],
   102	        [sp.diff(Hev_wall, dKsym), sp.diff(Hev_wall, dMsym)],
   103	    ])
   104	    sol_even = sp.solve(
   105	        [sp.Eq(K1_wall, 0), sp.Eq(Hev_wall, 0)],
   106	        [dKsym, dMsym],
   107	        dict=True,
   108	    )
   109	    assert_zero("wall-only even-gate determinant", wall_matrix.det() - sp.Rational(1, 27))
   110	    assert_nonzero("mutated wall-only determinant should fail", wall_matrix.det() + sp.Rational(1, 27))
   111	
   112	    # If the outgoing transfer remains isotropic at first order, Xi_load is driven only by D01.
   113	    Xi20 = sp.simplify(-D01_20 / D0)
   114	    Xi21 = sp.simplify(-D01_21 / D0)
   115	    Xi22 = sp.simplify(-D01_22 / D0)
   116	    Xibar, aXi, bXi = grouped_trace_anomaly(Xi20, Xi21, Xi22)
   117	
   118	    # Prefactor shifts for P0 = N0 / D0 with N01 = 0.
   119	    dP20 = sp.simplify(-N0 * D01_20 / D0**2)
   120	    dP21 = sp.simplify(-N0 * D01_21 / D0**2)
   121	    dP22 = sp.simplify(-N0 * D01_22 / D0**2)
   122	    Pbar, aP, bP = grouped_trace_anomaly(dP20, dP21, dP22)
   123	    if sol_even != [{dKsym: 0, dMsym: 0}]:
   124	        raise AssertionError(f'Wall-only even gates should solve trivially: {sol_even}')
   125	    assert_zero('Xi trace', Xibar)
   126	    assert_zero('Xi b=3a', bXi - 3*aXi)
   127	    assert_zero('prefactor trace', Pbar)
   128	    assert_zero('prefactor b=3a', bP - 3*aP)
   129	
   130	    lines: list[str] = []
   131	    lines.append("Weak-axisymmetric throat-action bridge\n")
   132	    lines.append("Assume a weak axisymmetric Y20 perturbation of the promoted wall action:\n")
   133	    lines.append("  mu_eta -> mu_eta + eps * delta_mu(w) Y20\n")
   134	    lines.append("  T_w    -> T_w    + eps * delta_Tw(w) Y20\n")
   135	    lines.append("  T_Omega-> T_Omega+ eps * delta_TO(w) Y20\n")
   136	    lines.append("  K_eta  -> K_eta  + eps * delta_Keta(w) Y20.\n")
   137	    lines.append("The exact grouped-lane signature from the Y20 triple overlap is\n")
   138	    lines.append("  lambda_(20) = 1, lambda_(21) = 1/2, lambda_(22) = -1.\n")
   139	    lines.append("\n")
   140	    lines.append("So any wall-sector shift X_A has the form X_A = X^(0) + eps * lambda_A * X1.\n")
   141	    lines.append(f"Grouped trace/anomaly of the wall inertia shift: Mbar={sp.sstr(Mbar)}, a_M={sp.sstr(aM)}, b_M={sp.sstr(bM)}\n")
   142	    lines.append(f"Grouped trace/anomaly of the wall stiffness shift: Kbar={sp.sstr(Kbar)}, a_K={sp.sstr(aK)}, b_K={sp.sstr(bK)}\n")
   143	    lines.append("Hence every first-order axisymmetric wall anisotropy obeys b = 3 a exactly.\n")
   144	    lines.append("\n")
   145	    lines.append("Wall-only contributions to the live weak-axisymmetric gates are\n")
   146	    lines.append(f"  K1_(20,21,22)   = ({sp.sstr(K1_gate_20)}, {sp.sstr(K1_gate_21)}, {sp.sstr(K1_gate_22)})\n")
   147	    lines.append(f"  H_even_(20,21,22) = ({sp.sstr(Hev_20)}, {sp.sstr(Hev_21)}, {sp.sstr(Hev_22)})\n")
   148	    lines.append("with generic lane formulas\n")
   149	    lines.append("  K1_wall = -delta_M + delta_K/9\n")
   150	    lines.append("  H_even,wall = 2 delta_M / 3 - delta_K / 27.\n")
   151	    lines.append(f"Solving K1_wall = 0 and H_even,wall = 0 gives: {sol_even}\n")
   152	    lines.append("Therefore pure wall anisotropy closes the even gates only on the trivial branch delta_K = delta_M = 0.\n")
   153	    lines.append("\n")
   154	    lines.append("If the outgoing-transfer bundle stays isotropic at first order (N01 = 0), then\n")
   155	    lines.append("  Xi_load^(A) = - D01^(A) / D0 = - eps * lambda_A * K1w / D0.\n")
   156	    lines.append(f"Its grouped defects are Xibar={sp.sstr(Xibar)}, a_Xi={sp.sstr(aXi)}, b_Xi={sp.sstr(bXi)}\n")
   157	    lines.append("so Xi_load again obeys b = 3 a.\n")
   158	    lines.append("\n")
   159	    lines.append("The induced prefactor shift dP0^(A) = -N0 D01^(A) / D0^2 has grouped defects\n")
   160	    lines.append(f"  Pbar={sp.sstr(Pbar)}, a_P={sp.sstr(aP)}, b_P={sp.sstr(bP)}\n")
   161	    lines.append("and again lies on the same weak-axisymmetric line b = 3 a.\n")
   162	    lines.append("\n")
   163	    lines.append("Conclusion: promoting S_Sigma gives a parent-level origin for the grouped signature (20,21,22) ~ (1,1/2,-1), but by itself it does not solve the full weak-axisymmetric 5PN gates. The Maxwell/mixed and support sectors still have to participate.\n")
   164	    lines.append("\nSTATUS: PASS\n")
   165	
   166	    print("".join(lines))

   169	if __name__ == "__main__":

     2	"""Parent throat action: weak-axisymmetric packet audit.
     3	
     4	This script verifies the exact wall-slope solve of the even gates once the

     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	import sympy as sp
    11	from sympy.physics.wigner import gaunt
    12	
    13	
    14	def assert_zero(label: str, expr: sp.Expr) -> None:
    15	    residue = sp.factor(sp.together(sp.simplify(expr)))
    16	    if residue != 0:
    17	        raise AssertionError(f"{label} failed: {sp.sstr(residue)}")
    18	
    19	
    20	def real_y20_square_ratio(m: int) -> sp.Expr:
    21	    base = sp.simplify(gaunt(2, 2, 2, 0, 0, 0))
    22	    if m != 0:
    23	        same_sign = sp.simplify(gaunt(2, 2, 2, 0, m, m))
    24	        if same_sign != 0:
    25	            raise AssertionError(f"Real-harmonic same-sign cross term should vanish for m={m}: {same_sign}")
    26	    return sp.simplify((sp.Integer(-1) ** m) * gaunt(2, 2, 2, 0, m, -m) / base)
    27	
    28	
    29	def main() -> None:
    30	    dKSigma, dMSigma = sp.symbols('dKSigma dMSigma')
    31	    B01, B21, B41 = sp.symbols('B01 B21 B41')
    32	    Z01, Z21, Z41 = sp.symbols('Z01 Z21 Z41')
    33	    N01, N0 = sp.symbols('N01 N0', nonzero=True)
    34	    KSigma, B0, Z0 = sp.symbols('KSigma B0 Z0', nonzero=True)
    35	
    36	    D0 = KSigma - B0 - Z0
    37	    D01 = dKSigma - B01 - Z01
    38	    D21 = -(dMSigma + B21 + Z21)
    39	    D41 = -(B41 + Z41)
    40	
    41	    K1 = sp.expand(D21 + D01 / sp.Integer(9))
    42	    H_even = sp.expand(D41 - sp.Rational(2, 3) * D21 - D01 / sp.Integer(27))
    43	    Xi1 = sp.expand(N01 / N0 - D01 / D0)
    44	
    45	    lam20 = real_y20_square_ratio(0)
    46	    lam21 = real_y20_square_ratio(1)
    47	    lam22 = real_y20_square_ratio(2)
    48	    assert_zero('Y20 overlap lane 20', lam20 - 1)
    49	    assert_zero('Y20 overlap lane 21', lam21 - sp.Rational(1, 2))
    50	    assert_zero('Y20 overlap lane 22', lam22 + 1)
    51	
    52	    coeff_matrix = sp.Matrix([
    53	        [sp.diff(K1, dKSigma), sp.diff(K1, dMSigma)],
    54	        [sp.diff(H_even, dKSigma), sp.diff(H_even, dMSigma)],
    55	    ])
    56	    assert_zero('even-gate solve determinant', coeff_matrix.det() - sp.Rational(1, 27))
    57	
    58	    sol = sp.solve([sp.Eq(K1, 0), sp.Eq(H_even, 0)], [dKSigma, dMSigma], dict=True)[0]
    59	    D01_comp = sp.simplify(D01.subs(sol))
    60	    D21_comp = sp.simplify(D21.subs(sol))
    61	    D41_comp = sp.simplify(D41.subs(sol))

    63	    closed_dK = B01 + Z01 + 27 * (B41 + Z41)
    64	    closed_dM = -(B21 + Z21) + 3 * (B41 + Z41)
    65	    assert_zero('dKSigma closed form', sol[dKSigma] - (B01 + Z01 + 27*(B41 + Z41)))
    66	    assert_zero('dMSigma closed form', sol[dMSigma] - (-(B21 + Z21) + 3*(B41 + Z41)))
    67	    assert_zero('compensated D01', D01_comp - 27*(B41 + Z41))
    68	    assert_zero('compensated D21', D21_comp + 3*(B41 + Z41))
    69	    assert_zero('compensated D41', D41_comp + B41 + Z41)
    70	    assert_zero('compensated Xi1', Xi1_comp - (N01/N0 - 27*(B41 + Z41)/(KSigma - B0 - Z0)))
    71

    80	    lines.append(f'Xi1 = {Xi1}')

    83	    lines.append(f'dMSigma_from_even_gates = {sol[dMSigma]}')
    84	    lines.append('')
    85	    lines.append(f'D01_on_compensated_branch = {D01_comp}')
    86	    lines.append(f'D21_on_compensated_branch = {D21_comp}')

    90	    lines.append(f'Xi1_on_compensated_branch = {Xi1_comp}')
